It controls epistemic status and containment, never evidence visibility or presumptive acceptance/rejection.","legacy_docket_assessment":{"adjudicable":false,"packet":null,"status":"invalid"},"packet_scope_relation":"exact_declared_pericope","reader_identity":[{"focus_ref":"102:1","identity_status":"legacy_unbound","packet_identity":null,"protocol":"focus-trace-hermetic-response-v4","reader_id":"reader_hft_a","trace_kind":"reconstructed"}]},"reader_synthesis_count":9,"source_present":true,"structured_insight_count":17,"unstructured_record_count":0},"identity":{"ayah_ref":"102:1","lane":"global","linguistic_source_ref":"102:1","surface_ref":"102:1","unit_kind":"numbered_ayah"},"primary_floor":{"source_ref":"102:1","target_tokens":[["Çoklukta",["102:1:2"]],["yarışmak",["102:1:2"]],["sizi",["102:1:1"]],["oyaladı",["102:1:1"]]],"text":"Çoklukta yarışmak sizi oyaladı."},"schema_version":"commentary-v5-hermetic-scope-packet-v1","scope":{"hft":{"assigned_record_count":9,"authoring_policy":{"every_assigned_record_requires_explicit_review":true,"parseable_hft_is_always_visible_to_authoring":true,"provenance_affects_qualification_not_visibility":true,"scope_mismatch_affects_lane_assignment_not_visibility":true,"unresolved_branch_citations_do_not_suppress_records":true},"authoring_status":"visible_with_provenance_qualification"},"lane_contract":"reader walks and cross-run publications with explicit trust labels","pericope":{"ayah_from":1,"ayah_to":8,"id":"s102-p01-001-008","label":"Whole surah","number":1,"refs":["102:1","102:2","102:3","102:4","102:5","102:6","102:7","102:8"]},"readiness":{"authoring_effect":"Docket readiness is reported for source provenance. HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000661/B001","root_001286/B002","root_001382/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_056c21f18c60d1de48f9","text":"1. **Distraction as competitive displacement.** Branch evidence: ل ه و B001, \"being diverted from a concern by another occupation\"; ك ث ر B002, \"outnumbering and rivalry in abundance\"; ق ب ر B001, grave; س ء ل B001, questioning. Structural cue: \"ألهاكم\" is immediately followed by the object \"التكاثر\" and then by the limit \"حتى زرتم المقابر\"; the final ayah adds interrogation. Assigned role: لهو supplies the displacement, تكاثر supplies the competing occupation, قبر/سأل supply the concern from which one was displaced. Abductive move: I infer the omitted displaced concern from the later grave and question rather than from the verb alone. Reading change: the ayah reads less as \"you enjoyed many things\" and more as \"a rivalry-system occupied you away from mortality and answerability.\"","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001286/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_15ff9efa271ae31a82e6","text":"2. **Abundance as vertical social positioning, not only having more.** Fixed `ك ث ر B002` explicitly includes outnumbering, boasting, and competition through status. The preceding `بِسْمِ` activates contextual `س م و B005` (raised designation) and, by dormant association, `س م و B007` (rivaling by vying upward). This adjacency gives the competition a vertical geometry: quantities are assigned the role of lifting names and rank over rivals. I infer that numerical or material superiority is being converted into public elevation; that conversion is not grammatically stated. `ٱلتَّكَاثُرُ` therefore reads as a social ranking process in which possessions or numbers are instruments for making the self loom higher.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001286/B001","root_001382/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_167958cd362a411053f1","text":"3. **The mill-mouth mechanism.** Branch evidence: ل ه و B003, \"casting grain into the mill mouth, with gift by likeness\"; ك ث ر B001, abundance and numerical increase; ج ح م B001, blazing fire/intense heat; ق ب ر B002, inward hiddenness. Structural cue: the first ayah's occupation moves into grave-enclosure and then fire-vision. Assigned role: تكاثر supplies material volume; لهو B003 supplies the process-image of being fed into a consuming mechanism. Abductive move: I supply the metaphorical arrow from \"that which is put into the mill\" to \"a life fed into consumption.\" Reading change: التكاثر reads as the very feedstock of a grinding course, not only the object that distracts.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001286/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_22388d91bfe619c3283c","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"102:1:2\",\"root_001286\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Sayısal üstünlük yarışı, kişileri, soyları ve ölüleri bile sayılabilir üstünlük malzemesine çevirebilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001286/B002","root_001286/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_26fbcd7fd5fe023f60d7","text":"2. **Abundance as a public scoreboard.** Branch evidence: ك ث ر B002, mutual boasting by number/wealth/status; ك ث ر B003, abundance attached to a person, speech, or claims; ر ء ي B005, showing off before people; ع ي ن B011, present cash; س م و B007, rivaling upward. Structural cue: the verbal form التكاثر is reciprocal/intensive, and the later seeing-language turns hidden claims into exposed realities. Assigned role: abundance becomes a visible social measure, not merely private possession. Abductive move: I infer that rivalry requires observers, comparison, and signs of possession. Reading change: the ayah becomes a critique of displayable surplus and status performance.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001286/B002","root_001382/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_4242b816a44a38ef5040","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"102:1:1\",\"root_001382\",[\"B001\"]],[\"102:1:2\",\"root_001286\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Çokluk yarışı, ölüm ve hesap gerçeğini dikkat alanından çıkaran bir perde kurar; isteyerek sürdürülen bu körlük sonunda zorunlu görüşle tersine çevrilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001286/B001","root_001382/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_5ea365bc4557ebc06df4","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"102:1:1\",\"root_001382\",[\"B003\"]],[\"102:1:2\",\"root_001286\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Her yeni artışın bir sonrakini isteyen öğütücü bir düzene besin olduğu ve bu düzenin insanı mezara kadar tükettiği okuması mümkündür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001382/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_6241ea3b9a30a96e9830","text":"1. **An attentional blindness that ends in compulsory sight.** Fixed `أَلْهَىٰكُمُ` anchors `ل ه و B001` (being occupied away from a concern), and fixed `ٱلتَّكَاثُرُ` anchors `ك ث ر B002` (mutual rivalry in abundance). The sequence runs from this occupation through graves and repeated future knowing to `لَتَرَوُنَّ ٱلْجَحِيمَ` in 102:6, where contextual `ر ء ي B001` supplies actual seeing. Diversion acts as a screen; rivalry is what keeps that screen in place. The packet supplies the shift from occupation to knowledge and sight, while I infer that the screened concern is the grave-bounded reality eventually made visible. The ayah thus reads as a reversible perceptual condition, not merely as wasted leisure: chosen attention is finally answered by forced vision.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001286/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_85beb9301183384104c4","text":"4. **Counting people through graves.** Branch evidence: ك ث ر B002, boasting by number/status; ر ح م B002, kinship bond; ق ب ر B001, cemetery/place of graves. Structural cue: the rivalry is followed directly by a plural cemetery, not by a marketplace or storehouse. Assigned role: kinship and the dead can become counted assets in the competitive field. Abductive move: I infer that the plural \"المقابر\" can activate a counting-place for lineages and bodies because التكاثر seeks numerical superiority. Reading change: the ayah may include not only wealth-rivalry but enlargement through persons, ancestry, and the dead.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001382/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_873951c1929f250b6c85","text":"3. **Exploratory: a mill-like demand for fresh input.** Fixed `ل ه و B003` preserves the material image of grain cast into a mill mouth, while fixed `ك ث ر B001` supplies repeated numerical increase. The causative verb followed by an iterative rivalry, then the terminal `حَتَّىٰ ... ٱلْمَقَابِرَ`, supports a throughput model: each increment becomes feed for a mechanism that demands another. I supply both the mill analogy and the arrow from added input to renewed appetite; the weak link is that `B003` is a nominal material branch rather than the direct verbal sense of `أَلْهَىٰكُمُ`. Under this activation, the ayah reads less like a single distraction and more like an impersonal process that consumes additions until its participants reach the graves.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001286/B002","root_001286/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_882b979846f2c909382c","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"102:1:2\",\"root_001286\",[\"B002\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"İnsanların önünde üstün görünmek için kurulan gösteri, sonunda gösterenlerin gözetlenip hesaba çekildiği bir maruziyete döner.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_a0fbedd7793c57b4bb8f","text":"102:2 first corrected a purely psychological reading by giving the fixed diversion a path and a grave-bound limit. The strongest later surprise was 102:6: `ج ح م B001` includes abundant piled embers, so the fixed `ك ث ر B001/B002` returns as an inverted surplus, while emphatic seeing reverses `ل ه و B001` from self-maintained inattention into compulsory perception.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001286/B002","root_001286/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_a89f57534495724e0039","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"102:1:2\",\"root_001286\",[\"B002\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Çokluk, özel mülkiyetten çok, sayıyı, serveti ve statü iddialarını insanların önünde ölçen bir toplumsal sıralama düzenine dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001286/B001","root_001286/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_ec94a00e8b66476288f0","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"102:1:2\",\"root_001286\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Aranan daha fazlası, ileride yığılmış korların dehşetli bolluğuyla tersine çevrilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_faf4e99bdca7aa4d69a1","text":"102:7 sharpened the public-scoreboard reading because عين B011 can mean present tangible wealth, bringing the rivalry from reputation into an audit-ready present. 102:8 then made the reversal explicit: what التكاثر had counted as advantage becomes the object of سؤال. The further surprise from 102:6 is that جحيم B003, the staring/glowing eye, makes social display recoil as exposure; the watchers of one another become the watched.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
